So take absolute value.\n\nSo | (n )*0 + (-n )*0 + (0.5 +n )*1 | / ( sqrt(n2 +n2 + (0.5 +n )2 )*1 )= |0.5 +n | /sqrt(2n2 + (0.5 +n )2 )\n\nSet equal to 0.5.\n\nAs above, leading to quadratic equation with no positive solutions.\n\nThus, again, no possible positive n. Hence, the problem as stated may have no solution, but as per problem statement, we need conditions on m and n. Therefore, maybe in different coordinates.\n\nBack to first principle:\n\nGiven EF⊥FD, which gives equation -t2 +mt'}]}
